add_namespace = cofpolitics

country_event = {
	id = cofpolitics.1
	title = cofpolitics.1.title
	desc = cofpolitics.1.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_map_france
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1.a
        ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { coalition_partner_var = token:radical_socialist }
			remove_from_coalition = yes
		}
	}
    option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1.b
        ai_chance = { base = 50 }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_rouge
			set_temp_variable = { coalition_partner_var = token:radical_socialist }
			remove_from_coalition = yes
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = cofpolitics.38
	title = cofpolitics.38.title
	desc = cofpolitics.38.desc
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_books
	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			country_event = { id = cofpolitics.39 days = 3 }
			country_event = { id = cofpolitics.40 days = 6 }

		clr_country_flag = CGSC_oppo
		clr_country_flag = CGSC_jaco
		clr_country_flag = CGSC_sore

	}

		name = cofpolitics.38.b

			country_event = { id = cofpolitics.51 days = 19 random_hours = 36 }
			country_event = { id = cofpolitics.52 days = 21 random_hours = 36 }
			country_event = { id = cofpolitics.53 days = 23 random_hours = 36 }

			if = {
				limit = { has_war_with = GER }
				country_event = { id = cofpolitics.54 days = 25 random_hours = 36 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { GER = { exists = no } }
				country_event = { id = cofpolitics.55 days = 25 random_hours = 36 }
			}
		}
	}

	id = cofpolitics.1000
	title = {
		text = cofpolitics.1000.title.a
		trigger = { check_variable = { agree_vote > 326 } }
	}
	title = {
		text = cofpolitics.1000.title.b
		trigger = { check_variable = { agree_vote < 327 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = cofpolitics.1000.desc.a
		trigger = { check_variable = { agree_vote > 326 } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = cofpolitics.1000.desc.b
		trigger = { check_variable = { agree_vote < 327 } }
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_trial_big
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1000.a
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		trigger = { check_variable = { agree_vote > 326 } }
	}	
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1000.b
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
		trigger = { check_variable = { agree_vote < 327 } }
		
	}	
}

		party_choice_effect = yes

		party_choice_effect = yes
	}	
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1001.d

        clr_country_flag = party_cost_economy
        clr_country_flag = party_cost_satisfaction
	}	
}

country_event = {
	id = cofpolitics.1002
	title = {
		text = cofpolitics.1002.title.preach
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach }
	}
	title = {
		text = cofpolitics.1002.title.argue
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue }
	}
	desc = {
		text = cofpolitics.1002.desc.preach
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach }
	}
	desc = {
		text = cofpolitics.1002.desc.argue
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue }
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_generic_trial_big
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1002.pa
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = has_preach_game } }
		else_if = { 
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach
				NOT = { has_country_flag = has_preach_game }
			}
			set_country_flag = has_preach_game 
		}

		set_variable = { game_turn = 1 }
		clear_array = preach_game
		clear_array = preach_game_p
		clear_array = preach_game_ai
		set_country_flag = preach_gaming

		parilament_choice_logic_rock = yes
	}	
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1002.pb
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = has_preach_game } }
		else_if = { 
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach
				NOT = { has_country_flag = has_preach_game }
			}
			set_country_flag = has_preach_game 
		}

		set_variable = { game_turn = 1 }
		clear_array = preach_game
		clear_array = preach_game_p
		clear_array = preach_game_ai
		set_country_flag = preach_gaming

		parilament_choice_logic_scissor = yes
	}	
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1002.pc
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = has_preach_game } }
		else_if = { 
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach
				NOT = { has_country_flag = has_preach_game }
			}
			set_country_flag = has_preach_game 
		}

		set_variable = { game_turn = 1 }
		clear_array = preach_game
		clear_array = preach_game_p
		clear_array = preach_game_ai
		set_country_flag = preach_gaming

		parilament_choice_logic_paper = yes
	}	
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1002.aa
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = has_argue_game } }
		else_if = { 
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue
				NOT = { has_country_flag = has_argue_game }
			}
			set_country_flag = has_argue_game 
		}

		set_variable = { game_turn = 1 }
		clear_array = argue_game
		clear_array = argue_game_p
		clear_array = argue_game_ai
		set_country_flag = argue_gaming

		parilament_choice_logic_rock = yes
	}	
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1002.ab
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = has_argue_game } }
		else_if = { 
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue
				NOT = { has_country_flag = has_argue_game }
			}
			set_country_flag = has_argue_game 
		}

		set_variable = { game_turn = 1 }
		clear_array = argue_game
		clear_array = argue_game_p
		clear_array = argue_game_ai
		set_country_flag = argue_gaming

		parilament_choice_logic_scissor = yes
	}	
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1002.ac
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		trigger = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = has_argue_game } }
		else_if = { 
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue
				NOT = { has_country_flag = has_argue_game }
			}
			set_country_flag = has_argue_game 
		}

		set_variable = { game_turn = 1 }
		clear_array = argue_game
		clear_array = argue_game_p
		clear_array = argue_game_ai
		set_country_flag = argue_gaming

		parilament_choice_logic_paper = yes
	}	
	option = {
		name = cofpolitics.1002.d
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		trigger = { 
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_preach }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = has_preach_game } 
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = parilament_choice_argue }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = has_argue_game } 
			}
		}
	}	
}
